## EBPM:EBPM-041

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-005.md#EBPM-EBPM-041">EBPM:EBPM-041</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EBPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EBPM-041</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Selective automation and interface suitability</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>RESEARCH_EXTRACTION_AND_CRITICISM_ADJUDICATION</p></dd><dt>Discovery family</dt><dd><p>B8</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>S027</p></li><li><p>S029</p></li><li><p>S045</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>An examined candidate is not a pre-approved obligation. Compare interface-level RPA, deeper integration, bounded process-aware agents and non-automation; test the actual changed work rather than relying on a tool category.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Repetitive digital work or coordination overhead suggests a technical intervention.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>Retain manual work or improve information/organisation when exception cost or integration fragility outweighs automation benefit.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Adequate inputs, known interfaces, operation/support capacity and a meaningful outcome test.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/30.md#r-30e66a77e1d42d7376547000c0c3e2246849d2b728b1ea40a03197e656cbb571">Complete shared assumptions · 30e66a77e1d4</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Automation executes delegated operations; it does not establish the legitimacy of the underlying business decision.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Users, technical operators and owners communicate interface changes, exceptions and consequences.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/46.md#r-465270a614b2cea3a127619cc3a464e620ee6374ed9dad93860e77643c48753f">Complete shared consumer · 465270a614b2</a></p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>The selected mechanism performs its bounded function under stated conditions, with business consequences evaluated separately.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>Fragile screen automation, accelerated flawed decisions, hidden exceptions and maintenance cost omitted from benefit claims.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Fragile screen automation, accelerated flawed decisions, hidden exceptions and maintenance cost omitted from benefit claims.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p>S029 is an agenda/editorial rather than a comparative effectiveness study; ## EBPM:EBPM-042

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-005.md#EBPM-EBPM-042">EBPM:EBPM-042</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EBPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EBPM-042</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>External-effect and completion reconciliation</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>RESEARCH_EXTRACTION_AND_CRITICISM_ADJUDICATION</p></dd><dt>Discovery family</dt><dd><p>B8</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>S002</p></li><li><p>S011</p></li><li><p>S027</p></li><li><p>S028</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>An examined candidate is not a pre-approved obligation. Stress propositions are not attributed to the whole field; composition properties are this researcher’s synthesis.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_WITH_ANALYTICAL_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/f7.md#r-f70ccba8c31cb80946f9e674907348e660df684a8a06a84bf1c7fc4a11fbdb77">Complete shared evidence limit · f70ccba8c31c</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Business/external obligation completion and receiving-side evidence</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>Declared completion corresponds to the agreed business outcome, or remaining uncertainty and responsibility are explicit.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Distinguish a local task or engine completion from completion of the obligation to a recipient or partner. Identify the external effects that matter and obtain appropriate receiving-side or operational evidence before declaring the business case complete.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>A process sends messages, transfers goods or money, changes external records or closes an obligation.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>A direct recipient confirmation or simple reconciliation may suffice; do not require a new distributed protocol for every process.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>An agreed business completion meaning, identifiable external dependencies and access to proportionate confirmation evidence.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/43.md#r-43240663199c2f7f9c88f8599bc257bf92e87d9d123e33babd6f6a236c767a97">Complete shared assumptions · 43240663199c</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Each actor controls its own commitments; a local owner cannot declare another party’s obligations fulfilled by fiat.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Communicate acceptance, rejection, missing effects and outstanding responsibility across interfaces.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/84.md#r-846caa43fc6b2115afd6cef6b1e119b97d1a8041d8dd8697d49faa201d6033ff">Complete shared consumer · 846caa43fc6b</a></p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>Declared completion corresponds to the agreed business outcome, or remaining uncertainty and responsibility are explicit.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>Local success masks failed delivery, duplicate effect, unresolved liability or manual repair.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Local success masks failed delivery, duplicate effect, unresolved liability or manual repair.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p>BPMN execution semantics cover a defined technical scope and may ignore non-operational elements; they do not prove organisational completion. S028 validates governance modelling, not all network outcomes.</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-001.md#EBPM-S002">EBPM:S002</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-002.md#EBPM-S011">EBPM:S011</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-003.md#EBPM-S027">EBPM:S027</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-003.md#EBPM-S028">EBPM:S028</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-005.md#EBPM-S041">EBPM:S041</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-006.md#EBPM-S054">EBPM:S054</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EBPM:R018</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R030</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R031</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R037</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R038</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R042</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R044</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>A direct recipient confirmation or simple reconciliation may suffice; do not require a new distributed protocol for every process.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. A justified existing substitute can supply the same function. Current native failure/authority/retention limits continue to apply.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Unresolved questions</dt><dd><ol><li><p>What evidence is affordable when the recipient cannot or will not provide confirmation?</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Domain lifecycle scope</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/ab.md#r-ab7dd31d15857da2024769b2174f1c6783b4d62f4cf04b2067134824f8f4e784">Complete shared domain lifecycle scope · ab7dd31d1585</a></p></dd><dt>Disposition rationale</dt><dd><p>Retained only in the stated criticism-tested form, with authority, evidence and consequence limits preserved. Stress propositions are not attributed to the whole field; composition properties are this researcher’s synthesis.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_WITH_ANALYTICAL_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>CONTEXT_DEPENDENT</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/76.md#r-769c896c98a598a9f18919516d6f43887e83de1f1bbad5bc1cb93fccc72b3879">Complete shared evidence limit · 769c896c98a5</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Interrupted work, outstanding obligations and authorised continuation/recovery</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>An interrupted case is not silently treated as completed, and an accountable decision governs continuation or resolution.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Where failed or interrupted work can leave consequential partial effects, ensure actors know how to pause, assess outstanding obligations and select an authorised continuation or recovery. The BPM-level requirement does not prescribe engine-specific retry, rollback or compensation algorithms.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Work can fail after consequential action or unsafe continuation is plausible.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>For a reversible low-impact task, manual stop-and-check with a responsible person may be sufficient.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Visibility of partial work, reachable responsible actors and feasible recovery options.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/df.md#r-dfacb02c97713cdbeff7043c4a3450be8c9b477d95aa40550a79d5e715a69c27">Complete shared assumptions · dfacb02c9771</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Operators may stop within delegated authority; reversal or compensation of another actor’s commitment requires the relevant authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Communicate what happened, what remains uncertain and who owns unfinished obligations.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/e7.md#r-e77cf324341ef206d8945fbed9251f6466671a30121c181c2e70e8787e39c80d">Complete shared consumer · e77cf324341e</a></p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>An interrupted case is not silently treated as completed, and an accountable decision governs continuation or resolution.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>Blind retry duplicates effects; a stop leaves recipients stranded; nominal rollback cannot undo human or external consequences.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Blind retry duplicates effects; a stop leaves recipients stranded; nominal rollback cannot undo human or external consequences.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p>The inspected specifications establish modelling/execution constructs, not universal organisational recovery success; S027 demonstrates limits of deployment evidence.</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-002.md#EBPM-S011">EBPM:S011</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-002.md#EBPM-S012">EBPM:S012</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-003.md#EBPM-S027">EBPM:S027</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-005.md#EBPM-S041">EBPM:S041</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-006.md#EBPM-S052">EBPM:S052</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-006.md#EBPM-S054">EBPM:S054</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-006.md#EBPM-S055">EBPM:S055</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EBPM:R018</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R022</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R028</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R032</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R042</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R047</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>For a reversible low-impact task, manual stop-and-check with a responsible person may be sufficient.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. A justified existing substitute can supply the same function. Current native failure/authority/retention limits continue to apply.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Unresolved questions</dt><dd><ol><li><p>How should continuity and protective stopping be prioritised for irreversible or time-critical services?</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Domain lifecycle scope</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/38.md#r-38298a948ad988782fbcbbb779e79875dd753c890d07c4c94f573c6df13fd8c3">Complete shared domain lifecycle scope · 38298a948ad9</a></p></dd><dt>Disposition rationale</dt><dd><p>A genuine option, not an unconditional requirement; selection depends on the stated trigger, prerequisites and cheaper alternatives. Process measures or monitoring are introduced or reviewed.</p></dd><dt>Consumer and authority</dt><dd><p>Monitoring analysts provide evidence; authorised owners or professionals decide interventions.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>A relevant observation results in a documented decision, inquiry or reasoned non-action rather than passive reporting.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>Alert overload, ritual dashboards, absent authority and incentives to suppress exceptions.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Alert overload, ritual dashboards, absent authority and incentives to suppress exceptions.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p>S036 explicitly differentiates technical from organisational/ecosystem action; S041 shows formal accounts serve particular consumers rather than exhaustive truth.</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-001.md#EBPM-S004">EBPM:S004</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-001.md#EBPM-S005">EBPM:S005</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-003.md#EBPM-S027">EBPM:S027</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-004.md#EBPM-S036">EBPM:S036</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-005.md#EBPM-S041">EBPM:S041</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EBPM:R023</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R027</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R031</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R032</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R033</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R035</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R036</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R040</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R047</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R050</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R051</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R061</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>Periodic case review or a simple threshold with a known responder may suffice; retire indicators with no current decision use.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. <a id="EBPM-EBPM-047"></a>
## EBPM:EBPM-047

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-005.md#EBPM-EBPM-047">EBPM:EBPM-047</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EBPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EBPM-047</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Feedback that can expose omitted work</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>RESEARCH_EXTRACTION_AND_CRITICISM_ADJUDICATION</p></dd><dt>Discovery family</dt><dd><p>B9</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>S006</p></li><li><p>S025</p></li><li><p>S035</p></li><li><p>S041</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>An examined candidate is not a pre-approved obligation. Stress propositions are not attributed to the whole field; composition properties are this researcher’s synthesis.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_WITH_ANALYTICAL_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/69.md#r-6988b75580aa8362476dcb30c2f72870a005c2b8e10661f4e6cab03004080ee8">Complete shared evidence limit · 6988b75580aa</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Accounts of performed work, variants and observations outside current models</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>A material discrepancy can lead to changed inquiry or explicit adjudication rather than exclusion as out of scope by definition.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Keep a route by which observations outside the current model—complaints, practitioner accounts, recipient experience and unlogged work—can challenge both the representation and the success measure. Do not let the model define all admissible evidence about its own adequacy.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>A process is evaluated primarily through records generated by its existing model or system.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>Occasional independent case walkthroughs and accessible complaint routes may suffice; exhaustive surveillance is not required.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>A channel for discrepant evidence, willingness to review it and access to actors outside the modelling team.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/1c.md#r-1c382b4278ed3a94b8e5d2f28353f7ea20cc3b6ee2e9b49fa0edc883a99a9389">Complete shared assumptions · 1c382b4278ed</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Evidence contributors may challenge a model; revision remains an authorised decision rather than automatic acceptance of every complaint.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Bring external or omitted observations to the people controlling the model, measures and process.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/86.md#r-86623d16782865c9bae8d0e4fa30e1737013542e39f4c263b6c06a72347fe75b">Complete shared consumer · 86623d167828</a></p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>A material discrepancy can lead to changed inquiry or explicit adjudication rather than exclusion as out of scope by definition.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>Self-confirming metrics, inaccessible complaint channels, cherry-picked external evidence and excessive investigative burden.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Self-confirming metrics, inaccessible complaint channels, cherry-picked external evidence and excessive investigative burden.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p>S041 shows formal descriptions can be useful while omitting performed work; S025 gives contextual workarounds, not a universal frequency estimate.</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-001.md#EBPM-S006">EBPM:S006</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-003.md#EBPM-S025">EBPM:S025</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-003.md#EBPM-S026">EBPM:S026</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-004.md#EBPM-S035">EBPM:S035</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-005.md#EBPM-S041">EBPM:S041</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EBPM:R029</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R036</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R047</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>Occasional independent case walkthroughs and accessible complaint routes may suffice; exhaustive surveillance is not required.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. A justified existing substitute can supply the same function.
